shaft_across_flat) / 2 = (8.0 - 6.5) / 2 = 0.75\n    flat_depth = (shaft_diameter - shaft_across_flat) / 2 + 0.15\n\n    # Create a D-shaped bore by cutting a circle and removing a segment\n    # Approach: create a cylinder for the round part, then cut the flat part\n\n    # The bore is a D-shape: circle with a flat side removed\n    # Flat faces +X, so we remove the +X side\n\n    # Full circular bore\n    round_bore = cylinder(bore_radius, bore_depth + 2)\n\n    # Rectangular cut for the flat side\n    # This removes the flat part from +X side\n    flat_cut = box(\n        bore_radius * 2,  # width (Y direction)\n        bore_radius * 2 + flat_depth + 1,  # depth (X direction, extends past bore)\n        bore_depth + 3,  # height (Z direction)\n        anchor=\"cc-\"\n    ).translate([bore_radius + flat_depth/2, 0, -0.5])\n\n    # Create D-shaped bore by subtracting the flat cut from the circle\n    d_bore = difference(round_bore, flat_cut)\n\n    # The bore needs to be positioned at the top center of the knob\n    d_bore = d_bore.translate([0, 0, knob_height - the base knob\n    grip = cylinder(knob_diameter / 2, knob_height)\n\n    # Add grip flats if enabled (increases grip width, improves torque transmission feel)\n    if grip_flats:\n        # Create flat sides on the outside for better grip\n        flat_width = grip_width - knob_diameter\n        flat_height = knob_height * 0.7\n\n        # Two flat protrusions opposite each other for grip\n        flat_size = flat_width / 2\n        flat_thick = 2.0\n\n        # Front flat\n        front_flat = box(\n            flat_size * 2,\n            flat_thick,\n            flat_height,\n            anchor=\"cc-\"\n        ).translate([0, knob_diameter/2 + flat_thick/2, knob_height*0.15])\n\n        # Back flat\n        back_flat = box(\n            flat_size * 2,\n            flat_thick,\n            flat_height,\n            anchor=\"cc-\"\n        ).translate([0, -(knob_diameter/2 + flat_thick/2), knob_height*0.15])\n\n        grip = union(grip, front_flat, back_flat)\n\n    # D-shaped bore - modeled as a circle with a flattened side\n    # The flat side faces +X (positive X direction)\n\n    # Calculate bore dimensions with clearance\n    # Add 0.15mm clearance all around for the nominal 0.3mm-grown stem to pass\n    bore_radius = (shaft_diameter / 2) + 0.15\n\n    # The flat cuts into the circle\n    # Distance from center to flat: (shaft_diameter - shaft_across_flat) / 2 = (8.0 - 6.5) / 2 = 0.75\n    flat_depth = (shaft_diameter - shaft_across_flat) / 2 + 0.15\n\n    # Create a D-shaped bore by cutting a circle and removing a segment\n    # Approach: create a cylinder for the round part, then cut the flat part\n\n    # The bore is a D-shape: circle with a flat side removed\n    # Flat faces +X, so we remove the +X side\n\n    # Full circular bore\n    round_bore = cylinder(bore_radius, bore_depth + 2)\n\n    # Rectangular cut for the flat side\n    # This removes the flat part from +X side\n    flat_cut = box(\n        bore_radius * 2,  # width (Y direction)\n        bore_radius * 2 + flat_depth + 1,  # depth (X direction, extends past bore)\n        bore_depth + 3,  # height (Z direction)\n        anchor=\"cc-\"\n    ).translate([bore_radius + flat_depth/2, 0, -0.5])\n\n    # Create D-shaped bore by subtracting the flat cut from the circle\n    d_bore = difference(round_bore, flat_cut)\n\n    # The bore needs to be positioned at the top center of the knob\n    d_bore = d_bore.translate([0, 0, knob_height - bore_depth/2 - 1])\n\n    # Cut the bore into the grip\n    knob = difference(grip, d_bore)\n\n    # Add bottom chamfer for printability\n    bottom_edges = edges().bottom().faces()\n    knob = chamfer(knob, 1.0, bottom_edges)\n\n    # Add top edge chamfer\n    top_edges = edges().top().faces()\n    knob = chamfer(knob, 0.5, top_edges)\n\n    # Polish all edges\n    knob = polish(knob)\n\n    return knob","new_string":"from nurb import *\n\n@part\ndef valve_knob(\n    shaft_diameter=8.0,\n    shaft_across_flat=6.5,\n    bore_depth=12.0,\n    knob_diameter=32.0,\n    knob_height=14.0,\n    grip_width=36.0,\n):\n    \"\"\"\n    Valve knob with D-shaped bore for D-shaft stem.\n\n    shaft_diameter: D-shaft diameter in mm\n    shaft_across_flat: D-shaft flat measurement in mm\n    bore_depth: how deep to bore into the knob\n    knob_diameter: main cylinder diameter for grip\n    knob_height: knob height when sitting on bed\n    grip_width: maximum width of grip feature at mid-height\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Main grip cylinder - the base knob\n    grip = Cylinder(knob_diameter / 2, knob_height)\n\n    # Add grip flats for better handling (two opposite flat protrusions)\n    # This increases the width and helps with torque transmission\n    flat_width = (grip_width - knob_diameter) / 2\n    flat_height = knob_height * 0.6\n    flat_thick = 2.0\n\n    # Front flat - adds width in +Y direction\n    front_flat = Box(\n        flat_width * 2,\n        flat_thick,\n        flat_height,\n        mode=Mode.CENTER\n    ) * Plane(origin=(0, 0, knob_height*0.2)).make_box()\n    front_flat = front_flat.moved(Location((0, knob_diameter/2 + flat_thick/2, knob_height*0.2)))\n\n    # Back flat - adds width in -Y direction\n    back_flat = Box(\n        flat_width * 2,\n        flat_thick,\n        flat_height,\n        mode=Mode.CENTER\n    )\n    back_flat = back_flat.moved(Location((0, -(knob_diameter/2 + flat_thick/2), knob_height*0.2)))\n\n    grip = grip + front_flat + back_flat\n\n    # D-shaped bore - modeled as a circle with a flattened side\n    # The flat side faces +X (positive X direction)\n\n    # Calculate bore dimensions\n    # The nominal stem has diameter 8.0 and across-flat 6.5\n    # The flat cuts into the circle by (8.0 - 6.5) / 2 = 0.75mm\n    # We need clearance for a 0.3mm grown stem to pass:\n    #   - grown diameter: 8.3mm -> bore radius needs ~4.3mm\n    #   - grown flat: 6.8mm -> allows for 0.75mm flat cut\n\n    bore_radius = 4.3  # for 0.3mm grown stem (8.3mm dia) with ~0.05mm clearance\n    flat_depth = 0.8  # depth of flat cut, slightly larger than nominal 0.75mm\n\n    # Create D-shaped bore\n    # Start with full circular bore\n    d_bore = Cylinder(bore_radius, bore_depth + 2)\n\n    # Cut away the flat side (+X direction)\n    # The flat is perpendicular to X-axis, removing the +X side\n    flat_cut = Box(\n        bore_radius * 2,  # width (Y direction)\n        bore_radius * 2,  # depth along original axis\n        bore_depth + 3,   # height (Z direction)\n        mode=Mode.CENTER\n    )\n    flat_cut = flat_cut.moved(Location((bore_radius + flat_depth/2, 0, -0.5)))\n\n    # Subtract the flat from the circular bore to create D-shape\n    d_bore = d_bore - flat_cut\n\n    # Position bore at top center of knob, open upward\n    d_bore = d_bore.moved(Location((0, 0, knob_height - bore_depth/2)))\n\n    # Cut the bore into the grip\n    knob = grip - d_bore\n\n    # Polish all exposed edges\n    knob = polish(knob, knob.edges(), 1.0)\n\n    return knob"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":8,"cache_creation_input_tokens":920,"cache_read_input_tokens":34208,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":920},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"927555df-5999-4bf9-8c65-2252d928f1d9","uuid":"64e2234b-3a38-4f5f-8f42-8a162886cb6f","timestamp":"2026-08-24T01:38:52.553Z","request_id":"req_011CeLiev1RAgPp9yeMhpJdx"}

the base knob\n    grip = Cylinder(knob_diameter / 2, knob_height)\n\n    # Add grip flats for better handling\n    # Make them overlap with the cylinder to ensure they form one solid\n    flat_width = grip_width - knob_diameter\n    flat_height = knob_height * 0.5\n    flat_thick = 2.5\n\n    # Create flat protrusions that overlap the cylinder\n    # Position them to overlap significantly so they merge into one piece\n    front_flat = Box(flat_width, flat_thick, flat_height)\n    front_flat = front_flat.translate((0, knob_diameter/2, knob_height*0.25))\n\n    back_flat = Box(flat_width, flat_thick, flat_height)\n    back_flat = back_flat.translate((0, -knob_diameter/2, knob_height*0.25))\n\n    # Union them into one solid\n    grip = grip + front_flat + back_flat\n\n    # D-shaped bore - modeled as a circle with a flattened side\n    # The flat side faces +X (positive X direction)\n\n    # Calculate bore dimensions\n    # The nominal stem has diameter 8.0 and across-flat 6.5\n    # The flat cuts into the circle by (8.0 - 6.5) / 2 = 0.75mm\n    # We need clearance for a 0.3mm grown stem to pass\n\n    bore_radius = 4.3  # for 0.3mm grown stem (8.3mm dia) with clearance\n    flat_depth = 0.8  # depth of flat cut\n\n    # Create D-shaped bore\n    # Start with full circular bore\n    d_bore = Cylinder(bore_radius, bore_depth + 2)\n\n    # Cut away the flat side (+X direction)\n    flat_cut = Box(\n        bore_radius * 2,\n        bore_radius * 2,\n        bore_depth + 3\n    )\n    flat_cut = flat_cut.translate((bore_radius + flat_depth/2, 0, -0.5))\n\n    # Subtract the flat from the circular bore to create D-shape\n    d_bore = d_bore - flat_cut\n\n    # Position bore at top center of knob, open upward\n    d_bore = d_bore.translate((0, 0, knob_height - bore_depth/2))\n\n    # Cut the bore into the grip\n    knob = grip - d_bore\n\n    # Polish all exposed edges\n    knob = polish(knob, knob.edges(), 1.0)\n\n    return knob","new_string":"from nurb import *\n\n@part\ndef valve_knob(\n    shaft_diameter=8.0,\n    shaft_across_flat=6.5,\n    bore_depth=12.0,\n    knob_diameter=30.0,\n    knob_height=14.0,\n    grip_lobes=4,\n):\n    \"\"\"\n    Valve knob with D-shaped bore for D-shaft stem.\n\n    shaft_diameter: D-shaft diameter in mm\n    shaft_across_flat: D-shaft flat measurement in mm\n    bore_depth: how deep to bore into the knob\n    knob_diameter: main cylinder diameter for basic grip\n    knob_height: knob height when sitting on bed\n    grip_lobes: number of lobes for gripping (0 for plain cylinder)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Main grip body - cylinder with lobed exterior for gripping\n    # Use a larger diameter to accommodate lobes that meet grip requirements\n    if grip_lobes > 0:\n        # Create an octagonal-like grip pattern\n        # Build from a cylinder and add raised lobes\n        grip = Cylinder(knob_diameter / 2, knob_height)\n\n        # Add 4 lobes around the cylinder for gripping\n        lobe_height = knob_height * 0.5\n        lobe_radius = 2.5  # protrusion from cylinder surface\n        lobe_width = 6.0\n\n        for i in range(grip_lobes):\n            angle = i * 360 / grip_lobes\n            # Create lobe positioned around the cylinder\n            lobe = Box(lobe_width, lobe_radius * 2, lobe_height)\n            lobe_x = (knob_diameter / 2 + lobe_radius) * cos(radians(angle))\n            lobe_y = (knob_diameter / 2 + lobe_radius) * sin(radians(angle))\n            lobe = lobe.translate((lobe_x, lobe_y, knob_height * 0.25))\n            grip = grip + lobe\n    else:\n        grip = Cylinder(knob_diameter / 2, knob_height)\n\n    # D-shaped bore - modeled as a circle with a flattened side\n    # The flat side faces +X (positive X direction)\n\n    # Calculate bore dimensions\n    # The nominal stem has diameter 8.0 and across-flat 6.5\n    # For 0.3mm grown stem to pass: diameter becomes 8.3mm\n    # Bore radius should be ~4.2mm to give clearance for the round part\n\n    bore_radius = 4.2\n    flat_depth = 0.85\n\n    # Create D-shaped bore\n    # Circular bore\n    d_bore = Cylinder(bore_radius, bore_depth + 2)\n\n    # Cut away the flat side (+X direction)\n    flat_cut = Box(\n        bore_radius * 2,\n        bore_radius * 2,\n        bore_depth + 3\n    )\n    flat_cut = flat_cut.translate((bore_radius + flat_depth/2, 0, -0.5))\n\n    # Subtract to create D-shape\n    d_bore = d_bore - flat_cut\n\n    # Position bore at top center of knob\n    d_bore = d_bore.translate((0, 0, knob_height - bore_depth/2))\n\n    # Cut the bore into the grip\n    knob = grip - d_bore\n\n    # Chamfer bottom edges for bed printing\n    bottom_face = knob.faces().sort_by(lambda f: f.center.z)[0]\n    knob = chamfer(knob, 1.0, bottom_face.edges())\n\n    # Polish remaining edges\n    knob = polish(knob, knob.edges(), 0.8)\n\n    return knob"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":8,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1816,"cache_read_input_tokens":43802,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1816},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"927555df-5999-4bf9-8c65-2252d928f1d9","uuid":"9a8abdb1-b555-47aa-a228-9fb6794a7b1d","timestamp":"2026-08-24T01:40:34.625Z","request_id":"req_011CeLin6X94isBRu15zYqTy"}

cylinder with lobed exterior for gripping\n    # Use a larger diameter to accommodate lobes that meet grip requirements\n    if grip_lobes > 0:\n        # Create an octagonal-like grip pattern\n        # Build from a cylinder and add raised lobes\n        grip = Cylinder(knob_diameter / 2, knob_height)\n\n        # Add 4 lobes around the cylinder for gripping\n        lobe_height = knob_height * 0.5\n        lobe_radius = 2.5  # protrusion from cylinder surface\n        lobe_width = 6.0\n\n        for i in range(grip_lobes):\n            angle = i * 360 / grip_lobes\n            # Create lobe positioned around the cylinder\n            lobe = Box(lobe_width, lobe_radius * 2, lobe_height)\n            lobe_x = (knob_diameter / 2 + lobe_radius) * cos(radians(angle))\n            lobe_y = (knob_diameter / 2 + lobe_radius) * sin(radians(angle))\n            lobe = lobe.translate((lobe_x, lobe_y, knob_height * 0.25))\n            grip = grip + lobe\n    else:\n        grip = Cylinder(knob_diameter / 2, knob_height)\n\n    # D-shaped bore - modeled as a circle with a flattened side\n    # The flat side faces +X (positive X direction)\n\n    # Calculate bore dimensions\n    # The nominal stem has diameter 8.0 and across-flat 6.5\n    # For 0.3mm grown stem to pass: diameter becomes 8.3mm\n    # Bore radius should be ~4.2mm to give clearance for the round part\n\n    bore_radius = 4.2\n    flat_depth = 0.85\n\n    # Create D-shaped bore\n    # Circular bore\n    d_bore = Cylinder(bore_radius, bore_depth + 2)\n\n    # Cut away the flat side (+X direction)\n    flat_cut = Box(\n        bore_radius * 2,\n        bore_radius * 2,\n        bore_depth + 3\n    )\n    flat_cut = flat_cut.translate((bore_radius + flat_depth/2, 0, -0.5))\n\n    # Subtract to create D-shape\n    d_bore = d_bore - flat_cut\n\n    # Position bore at top center of knob\n    d_bore = d_bore.translate((0, 0, knob_height - bore_depth/2))\n\n    # Cut the bore into the grip\n    knob = grip - d_bore\n\n    # Chamfer all edges for smooth printing\n    knob = polish(knob, knob.edges(), 1.0)\n\n    return knob","new_string":"from nurb import *\n\n@part\ndef valve_knob(\n    shaft_diameter=8.0,\n    shaft_across_flat=6.5,\n    bore_depth=12.0,\n    knob_diameter=34.0,\n    knob_height=14.0,\n):\n    \"\"\"\n    Valve knob with D-shaped bore for D-shaft stem.\n\n    shaft_diameter: D-shaft diameter in mm\n    shaft_across_flat: D-shaft flat measurement in mm\n    bore_depth: how deep to bore into the knob\n    knob_diameter: main cylinder diameter for grip\n    knob_height: knob height when sitting on bed\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Main grip body - simple cylinder\n    # 34mm diameter gives 34mm width at mid-height (meets 28mm minimum)\n    # and provides 17mm radius (exceeds 12% expansion requirement)\n    grip = Cylinder(knob_diameter / 2, knob_height)\n\n    # D-shaped bore - modeled as a circle with a flattened side\n    # The flat side faces +X (positive X direction)\n\n    # Calculate bore dimensions\n    # Stem: diameter 8.0mm, across-flat 6.5mm\n    # Flat depth: (8.0 - 6.5) / 2 = 0.75mm\n    #\n    # For fit requirements:\n    # - 0.3mm grown stem (8.3mm dia, 6.8mm flat) must PASS\n    # - 1.0mm grown stem (9.0mm dia, 7.5mm flat) must JAM\n    #\n    # Bore radius for 0.3mm grown stem: need ~4.2mm radius + clearance\n    # This allows diameter of ~8.4mm at the round side (just barely fits 8.3mm)\n    # With flat positioned to match the stem geometry\n\n    bore_radius = 4.25\n    flat_depth = 0.8\n\n    # Create D-shaped bore\n    # Start with circular bore\n    d_bore = Cylinder(bore_radius, bore_depth + 2)\n\n    # Subtract the flat cut to create D-shape\n    # The flat cuts into the +X side of the circle\n    flat_cut = Box(bore_radius * 2, bore_radius * 2, bore_depth + 3)\n    flat_cut = flat_cut.translate((bore_radius + flat_depth / 2, 0, -0.5))\n\n    d_bore = d_bore - 0.3mm grown stem (8.3mm dia, 6.8mm flat) must PASS with clearance\n    # - 1.0mm grown stem (9.0mm dia, 7.5mm flat) must JAM (not pass)\n    # - Torque: must model the flat to transmit torque\n    #\n    # Strategy: Create bore as a rounded box with one flat side\n    # This avoids the sharp concave junction of a pure D-bore\n\n    # Bore opening radius\n    bore_radius = 4.2\n    flat_depth = 0.8\n\n    # Create the bore as a box with one side flattened\n    # This naturally creates smooth edges instead of sharp junctions\n    bore_width = bore_radius * 2\n    bore_height = bore_depth\n\n    # Main bore body - 6.5) / 2 = 0.75mm\n    #\n    # For fit requirements:\n    # - 0.3mm grown stem (8.3mm dia, 6.8mm flat) must PASS\n    # - 1.0mm grown stem (9.0mm dia, 7.5mm flat) must JAM\n    #\n    # Bore radius for 0.3mm grown stem: need ~4.2mm radius + clearance\n    # This allows diameter of ~8.4mm at the round side (just barely fits 8.3mm)\n    # With flat positioned to match the stem geometry\n\n    bore_radius = 4.3\n    flat_depth = 0.75\n\n    # Create a bore that fits the D-shaft with proper clearances\n    # Rather than using a sharp D-shape, create the bore by subtracting\n    # from the interior to avoid sharp concave edges\n\n    bore_radius = 4.3\n    flat_depth = 0.75\n\n    # Start with the knob body\n    knob = grip\n\n    # Create bore cavity using a cylindrical tool path\n    # Cut from the top, creating a bore opening\n    bore_cavity = Cylinder(bore_radius, bore_depth)\n\n    # Subtract the flat portion to create D-shape in the bore\n    flat_cut = Box(\n        bore_radius * 1.6,\n        bore_radius * 2,\n        bore_depth\n    )\n    flat_cut = flat_cut.translate((bore_radius + flat_depth / 2, 0, 0))\n\n    bore_cavity = bore_cavity - flat_cut\n\n    # Position and cut into knob from the top\n    bore_cavity = bore_cavity.translate((0, 0, knob_height - bore_depth / 2))\n\n    # Perform the subtraction to create the bore\n    knob = knob - bore_cavity\n\n    # Apply fillet/chamfer only to external edges using polish\n    # Avoid applying polish to internal bore edges that would create issues\n    external_edges = knob.edges().filter_by(lambda e: e.geom_type != \"Line\")\n    knob = polish(knob, external_edges, 0.8)\n\n    return knob"}
